compare.file_rows - idx_compare_file_rows_market_price: CREATE INDEX idx_compare_file_rows_market_price ON compare.file_rows USING btree (price) WHERE ((price > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(TRIM(BOTH FROM raw_name), ''::text) <> ''::text))
compare.file_rows - idx_compare_file_rows_market_sort: CREATE INDEX idx_compare_file_rows_market_sort ON compare.file_rows USING btree ((
CASE
    WHEN ((COALESCE(discount, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(discount, (0)::numeric) < (100)::numeric)) THEN discount
    ELSE (0)::numeric
END) DESC, (
CASE
    WHEN (COALESCE(price_after_discount, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN price_after_discount
    WHEN ((COALESCE(discount, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(discount, (0)::numeric) < (100)::numeric)) THEN round(((price * (100.0 - discount)) / 100.0), 2)
    ELSE price
END)) WHERE ((price > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(TRIM(BOTH FROM raw_name), ''::text) <> ''::text))
